## G0 — VM-028 atomic grocery replacement

- Status: **Attempt 2 verification in progress**, following Sara's explicit correction approval. PR: [#10](https://github.com/sararh95/VioMacro/pull/10), draft and unmerged. Round-1 stop/verdict below is historical; the resume section above owns current progress. No group after G0 has started.
- Source/test commit: `84df095a4487c795fa11e633126af4dc824730c0`, pushed normally. CI check **passed**, 1m44s: https://github.com/sararh95/VioMacro/actions/runs/39851061648/job/120077030929. PR reports mergeable, no formal review decision; no merge attempted.

- Bugs: VM-028 only. G1 retains quantity/scaling/classification changes.
- Root cause: `src/features/groceries/shopping-generator.ts:52` clears generated groceries before checking plan recipe IDs (`:64`), recipe reads (`:71`), aggregation, or independent replacement writes (`:160-163`). `src/features/groceries/shoppingService.ts:138-155` clears all generated weeks when no plan ID is supplied, using separate delete batches (`:110-116`).
- Pattern search: `generateGroceryList` has one UI caller, `src/features/meal-planning/MealPlanner.tsx:856`; its refresh happens only after success. Generated replacement has no other caller. Manual add via `src/app/providers/store.tsx:397-401` and `src/features/groceries/ShoppingList.tsx:66` must remain unchanged. General clear and checked-clear are separate user actions; retain their behavior.
- Planned source files: shopping-generator.ts and shoppingService.ts. Add a dedicated regression test covering the actual generator/service, and extend emulator flow coverage if needed. Documentation: this log, bug ledger, QA ledger and grocery contract evidence.
- Intended behavior: validate/build every replacement before any write; empty/non-addable inputs leave saved items untouched. Query existing generated items, preserve checked state by normalized name+unit, preflight strictly fewer than 500 writes, then one atomic writeBatch. Preserve existing all-generated refresh scope and manual/legacy items. Reject oversized replacements before mutation; do not split an atomic refresh across batches.
- Planned tests: empty/no-ID plan; missing recipes/no ingredients; recipe read failure; malformed ingredient/build failure; commit failure; mixed manual/generated items; checked name/unit match; cross-week generated replacement; under-500 boundary and oversized rejection. Source mutation check required during Reviewer 1.

## G1 — Ingredient amounts

Status: **attempt 1 resumed after Sara's range decision**, from merged main `dd5f6fb`; branch `codex/fix-g1-ingredient-amounts`, draft [PR #11](https://github.com/sararh95/VioMacro/pull/11). Bugs: VM-001, VM-006, VM-007, VM-008, VM-011 and related VM-031. Range-policy history below is retained; current policy is the resume entry above.

Root causes/pattern search: generator aggregates recipe amounts per meal without dividing by recipe servings (`shopping-generator.ts:118-172`); legacy/structured parsing is duplicated at `:14-80` and `RecipeDetail.tsx:24-37`; quantity formatter rounds fractional parts independently (`src/shared/format-quantity.ts:28-32`); GuidedCooking displays legacy `ing.amount` only (`:149`); quick-add duplicates numeric parsing (`ShoppingList.tsx:31-46`); substring aisle matching misclassifies eggplant/black pepper (`shopping-generator.ts:7-10`, shared quick-add caller `ShoppingList.tsx:49`). SmartText receives scaled ingredient display from RecipeDetail (`src/shared/smart-text.tsx:83-84`); it performs no separate quantity math. RecipeForm's serialization/decimal defects remain G2. Search `src/` and `functions/src/` found no backend ingredient-amount parser; AI request projection remains G5. Pantry classification/parser remains outside this group's approved scope.

Expected files: new shared ingredient module for parse/scale/format/aggregate; generator, RecipeDetail, GuidedCooking, the quantity-formatter compatibility entry point and Grocery quick-add/display; meaningful unit/actual-source/component/emulator tests plus ledgers. Planned tests: recipe-servings division across five meals, legacy/structured/missing/zero/decimal/fraction/Unicode forms, range and qualitative preservation without invented precision, formatting carry, detail/SmartText/cooking parity, token-based aisle classification for generator and quick-add, reload and retained G0 atomic safety.

Planning constraint to resolve before implementation: `firestore.rules:233-250` permits only the fixed grocery keys and a single numeric `quantity`; `shoppingService.ts:8-9` has the same single-number model. Correctly preserving ranges such as `2–3 cloves` needs either a decided projection into one shopping quantity or a separately approved schema/rules migration. The existing parser currently leaks the upper endpoint into `unit`, which is the VM-008 defect. No existing upper/lower-bound shopping policy was found in the contracts/roadmap/report; the report explicitly says not to invent precision. No rule/index edit or deployment is authorized. Reproduce on this branch and record the concrete range/schema evidence before requesting a product decision; do not conceal a range inside an unrelated field.

Fresh Chrome repro on `dd5f6fb`, demo account/current-week five lunches: Add to List → Grocery displays Chicken120oz, Rice10cups and Garlic10 -3cloves, with Eggplant in Proteins and Black pepper in Produce (VM-001/008/011). DOM plus visually inspected desktop screenshot: `_local/qa/fix-loop-2026-10-22/g1/grocery-range-baseline.txt/.png`; Firestore rows in `range-model-evidence.json`. VM-006/007/031 and the remaining fraction cases have not yet been repeated in G1; retain them open, not Cannot reproduce.

Emulator-only model probe attempted (a) quantity string `2–3`, (b) quantity2 plus quantityMax3. ## G0 Reviewer 1 — exact published verdict

## Reviewer 1 (correctness)

Reviewed head: `84df095a4487c795fa11e633126af4dc824730c0`.

**REQUEST CHANGES**

1. **[P1 — serious data-safety issue] Reject malformed/nonaddable quantities before replacing saved groceries.** `src/features/groceries/shoppingService.ts:108` accepts zero, while `src/features/groceries/shopping-generator.ts:109` converts malformed structured quantities to zero with `parseFloat(ing.quantity) || 0`. In an independent synthetic-I/O reproduction, I seeded a checked generated Rice item and a separate manual control, then generated from `{ item: 'Invalid Rice', quantity: 'not-a-number', unit: 'cups' }`. The operation returned `{ success: true, count: 1 }`, committed two writes, deleted the saved checked generated item, and replaced it with an unchecked zero-quantity item; the manual control survived. A zero-only recipe and a recipe containing both valid and malformed quantities also committed replacements. Firestore rules permit zero quantities, so the server does not reject these payloads. This violates the required no-mutation behavior for invalid/nonaddable input and leaves a serious data-loss path in VM-028. Validate quantities before lossy coercion/aggregation and leave the saved list untouched when the replacement has no addable ingredients; add regressions for malformed, zero-only, and mixed valid/invalid input. Servings scaling need not change.

The fix addresses the original delete-before-build sequence and uses one batch below 500 writes. I found one production generation caller, which reaches the new helper.

Independent mutation evidence, Node v22.23.4, task-owned scratch copy: unchanged new tests with only the two source files reverted to base `a4d0bc7de3b856d7581922510b713bbbd99b135b` failed **7/9** (2 passed); restoring the reviewed source passed **9/9**. Additional adversarial probes reproduced the three unsafe replacement cases above; the legacy fraction plus decimal control passed. No PR checkout edits, commits, pushes, deploys, production access, or live AI calls.

Non-blocking nits: none.

Scratch evidence preserved at C:\Users\sarar\.codex\review-scratch\pr10-reviewer1-059b43d7ea7643c184b19fbc77568223. No PR source edits were made by the reviewer.

# Reviewer 1 (correctness)

REQUEST CHANGES

Reviewed frozen head `f483eb0b22dba5a0f66c94fda446eb8f6c794945` against base `dd5f6fb8019b4227c94bcf2e8be70f1aa124d2e9`. Frozen app/test patch SHA256: `dccf318a6b38988c6bcd7661bd9fb23265cd61dc97fd5f7a56f296c1801d0828`. Review was independent of author logs, PR body and prior reviews.

Blocking finding:

1. **[P2] Apply countable rounding to the recipe form's `Unit` value** — `src/shared/ingredients.ts:129-130` (and its unit aliases at lines 112-118). `RecipeForm` exposes `Unit` through `ALL_UNITS` (`src/shared/constants.ts:2`), but normalization leaves it as `unit`, which `countable()` does not recognize. A four-serving recipe with `{ item: 'Eggplant', quantity: '3', unit: 'Unit' }`, planned for five meals, persists **3.75 unit** rather than the required **4** whole items. I reproduced this through the actual `generateGroceryList` and persistence-service harness; the independent assertion fails with `3.75 !== 4`. ## Reviewer 1 (correctness)

**REQUEST CHANGES** — reviewed exact head `6432916f32c88b0ff09b23367429d22600de1e5f` against `dd5f6fb8019b4227c94bcf2e8be70f1aa124d2e9`. Frozen app/test patch SHA256: `0b64d115fff544fbbe9374c298a5f59d34a5c875c12a2a325a99834177d6217e`.

1. **[P2] Canonicalize package placement before deriving grocery identity — `src/shared/ingredients.ts:127` (used by line 131).** Both `(14 oz) can` and `can (14 oz)` are supported descriptions of the same package, but normalization produces `(14 oz) cans` and `cans (14 oz)`, so aggregation and checked-state preservation treat them as different ingredients. Using the current tests' unchanged actual generator/persistence harness, a checked Tomatoes row with unit `(14 oz) CANS` refreshed from `{ item: 'Tomatoes', quantity: '1', unit: 'can (14 oz)' }` becomes unchecked. A four-serving recipe containing `{ item: 'Tomatoes', amount: '1 (14 oz) can' }` and `{ item: 'Tomatoes', quantity: '1', unit: 'can (14 oz)' }`, planned for one portion, writes two 1-can rows. The combined need is 0.5 of the same 14-ounce can, so final count rounding should produce one 1-can row. Normalize supported package placement for identity before aggregation, retain different sizes as distinct, and cover cross-placement checked preservation and aggregation. Reviewer 1 (correctness)

Nonblocking nits / limits:
- P3, `src/shared/ingredients.ts:210`: quantities near but not exactly 1 can round to the displayed number 1 while retaining a plural (for example 0.999 cups displays `1 cups`). This is existing main behavior and is outside the reported exact numeric-1 fix; source-only observation.
- P3, `tests/e2e/emulator-auth.spec.ts:6310`: G14 checks and reloads a generated item but does not exercise the requested uncheck/recheck sequence or quick-add one cup and one packaged can. The shared caller and helper cases cover their labels, but those exact user flows remain unproven in this independent run. This is a coverage limitation, not a proven runtime regression.
